# 1852 new apps — 2026-11-26

Window: apps first found between 2026-11-25 21:01 UTC (22:01 Europe/Warsaw) and 2026-11-26 20:41 UTC (21:41 Europe/Warsaw) and released since 2026-11-24 21:01 UTC (22:01 Europe/Warsaw), plus apps found earlier whose release date arrived in that span. Date-only release dates count by calendar day in America/Los_Angeles. Storefronts: us.
